My supervisor shows me reports that don’t back that up, but I still can’t shake the feeling.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time when you didn't think people were plotting against you?\n\nPatient: Not really lately. I mean, the main reason I came in is I can’t shake it — that people at work, and maybe some vendors, are tracking my buys or messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid. Even when my supervisor shows me reports that don’t prove it, I still keep thinking it.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that your belief about people tracking you and messing with launch numbers is something that most people would find hard to believe?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess most people would probably think it sounds hard to believe. But to me it feels real. I can’t shake the feeling that people at work, and maybe vendors too, are tracking what I buy and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid, even when my supervisor shows me reports that don’t prove it.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been thinking that people at work and vendors are plotting against you?\n\nPatient: About ten months, I think. It wasn’t just a one-time thing — it’s been going on for roughly that long.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief that people are tracking you and messing with launch numbers is based on facts or is it just a feeling you have?\n\nPatient: I mean… I know I don’t have solid proof. My supervisor has shown me reports that don’t really back it up. But it still feels like it’s happening — like people at work, maybe vendors too, are tracking my buys and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid. I can’t shake it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief that people are tracking you and messing with launch numbers could be wrong?\n\nPatient: I mean… I guess it could be wrong. I don’t have proof, and my supervisor showed me reports that don’t really prove it. But it still feels real to me, like people at work and some vendors are tracking what I buy and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid. I just can’t shake it.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of time where you didn't believe people were plotting against you since this all started ten months ago?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. Since it started, that feeling has pretty much stayed there. Sometimes it’s quieter, but I still keep thinking people at work or vendors are tracking my buys and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid, even when I’m shown reports that don’t prove it.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that the idea of people tracking what you buy and messing with launch numbers to make you look bad is something that could happen in real life?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I think it could happen. People at work and vendors do see buying stuff and launch numbers, so it doesn’t feel impossible to me. I know I don’t have proof, and my supervisor says the reports don’t show it, but I still can’t shake the feeling that they’re using that to make me look bad.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a moment, even if just briefly, where you didn't believe people were plotting against you since this feeling started?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I might have moments where I try to tell myself maybe I’m overthinking it, especially when my supervisor shows me the reports, but I still feel like people at work or vendors are tracking my buys and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid. It doesn’t really go away.\n\nDoctor: Do you still believe people at work and vendors are plotting against you right now?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I still believe it. I know my supervisor keeps showing me reports that don’t prove anything is being messed with, but I can’t shake it. It still feels like people at work, and maybe some vendors, are tracking what I buy and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid.\n\nDoctor: Do you still think people are plotting against you when you're alone and not at work?\n\nPatient: Yeah… even when I’m alone, I still think about it. It’s mostly tied to work, like the buys and the launch numbers, but I don’t have to be there for the feeling to stay with me. I’ll be at home and still feel like people at work or some vendors are messing with things to make me look stupid, even though my supervisor says the reports don’t show it.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been a time since these feelings started where you didn't think about people plotting against you for at least a few days in a row?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think I’ve gone a few days without thinking about it. It’s kind of always there in the background. Even if I’m trying to focus on something else, I still come back to feeling like people at work or vendors are tracking my buys and messing with the numbers to make me look bad.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that the idea of people tracking what you buy and messing with launch numbers to make you look bad is something that could happen in a normal, everyday situation?\n\nPatient: I mean… I guess it could happen, like people can track orders and numbers at work. But the way I feel about it is stronger than just normal work stuff. I really feel like they’re using my buys and the launch numbers to make me look stupid, even when my supervisor shows me reports that don’t prove it.\n\nDoctor: Would you describe the idea that people at work and vendors are tracking your buys and messing with launch numbers as strange or unbelievable to most people?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I know most people would probably say it sounds unbelievable, or like I’m reading too much into it. But to me it feels real. I can’t shake it — like people at work and some vendors are tracking my buys and messing with the launch numbers so I look stupid, even when my supervisor shows me reports that don’t back that up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that your belief about people tracking you and messing with launch numbers is a fact or just your interpretation of things?\n\nPatient: It feels like a fact to me, honestly. I know other people keep saying the reports don’t prove it, but I can’t shake it. It really feels like people at work and some vendors are tracking my buys and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible for you to change your mind about people tracking you and messing with launch numbers if you were shown more evidence against it?\n\nPatient: I mean… maybe, but honestly I don’t know. It still feels like people at work and some vendors are tracking my buys and messing with the launch numbers to make me look stupid.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any chance that your feeling of being tracked and plotted against could be a product of your own mind rather than something that's actually happening?\n\nPatient: I guess there’s a chance, yeah… but it doesn’t feel that way to me.
